BEGIN Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.actual_tail_from_ray
Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.actual_tail_from_ray : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (n D : Nat) (p : Bool) (s : Heraclitus.State) (F c h shift outer₁ outer₂ : Int) (levels : Nat) (B : List Int),
      p = Bool.true ∧ 0 < a n ∨ p = Bool.false ∧ a n < 0 →
        s.current = Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.ray p (a n) →
          s.current = F + c →
            (∀ (x : Int),
                (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a n).used x →
                  Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.SameSide x (a n) → s.used (Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.ray p x)) →
              (∀ (x : Int),
                  s.used x →
                    x < F ∨
                      x - F ∈ B ∨
                        Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower h levels (x - F - shift) ∨ x - F = outer₁ ∨ x - F = outer₂) →
                (Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead c B).length ≤ 53 →
                  Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a n D ≤ 55 + 6 * levels
'Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.actual_tail_from_ray' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.ray_injective
Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.ray_injective : ∀ (p : Bool) {x y : Int},
  Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.ray p x = Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.ray p y → x = y
'Heraclitus.ActualTailCover.ray_injective' depends on axioms: [propext]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.crossing_error_bounds
Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.crossing_error_bounds : ∀ (m n r A delta : Int),
  7 ≤ m →
    n = 4 * m + delta →
      2 * r = 7 * m - 1 + 2 * A →
        -3 ≤ A ∧ A ≤ 3 →
          -1 ≤ delta ∧ delta ≤ 12 →
            0 < n ∧
              4 * m - 1 ≤ n ∧
                -112 ≤ 4 * Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.twiceDeviation n r - 3 * n ∧
                  4 * Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.twiceDeviation n r - 3 * n ≤ 27
'Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.crossing_error_bounds' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.crossing_identity
Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.crossing_identity : ∀ (m n r A delta : Int),
  7 ≤ m →
    n = 4 * m + delta →
      2 * r = 7 * m - 1 + 2 * A →
        -3 ≤ A ∧ A ≤ 3 →
          -1 ≤ delta ∧ delta ≤ 12 →
            0 < n ∧ 0 ≤ 2 * r - n ∧ 4 * Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.twiceDeviation n r - 3 * n = 8 * A - 4 - 7 * delta
'Heraclitus.CrossingArithmetic.crossing_identity' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END

  2 * C = m - 1 ∧ 2 * D = 7 * m - 1 →
    0 ≤ m →
      4 * m - 1 ≤ n →
        C - 100 ≤ r →
          r ≤ C + 2 * m + 4096 → n ≤ D + r + 7 + t → t ≤ 55 + 6 * L → 8 * r - 7 * n ≤ 32771 ∧ n - 8 * r ≤ 28737 + 6 * L
'Heraclitus.DescentBottomBound.defects' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

    4 * m - 1 ≤ n → r ≤ D + 4096 → n ≤ D + r + 7 + t → t ≤ 4 → 8 * r - 7 * n ≤ 32771 ∧ n - 8 * r ≤ 91

BEGIN Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.actual_walk
Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.actual_walk : ∀ (P : Nat → Heraclitus.State → Prop) (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (base : Nat) (w : List Int),
      Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w →
        Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.All P base (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w →
          ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ w.length → P (base + i) (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + i))
'Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.actual_walk' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.append
Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.append : ∀ (P : Nat → Heraclitus.State → Prop) (base : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.State)
  (u v : List Int),
  Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.All P base s u →
    Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.All P (base + u.length) (Heraclitus.execute s u) v →
      Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.All P base s (u ++ v)
'Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.append' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.blocks
Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.blocks : ∀ (P : Nat → Heraclitus.State → Prop) (base : Nat) {s t z : Heraclitus.State}
  (first : Heraclitus.SignedBlock s t) (second : Heraclitus.SignedBlock t z),
  Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.All P base s first.word →
    Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.All P (base + first.word.length) t second.word →
      Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.All P base s (first.then second).word
'Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.blocks' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.valid_words_unique
Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.valid_words_unique : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (u v : List Int),
  Heraclitus.SignedValidWord s u → Heraclitus.SignedValidWord s v → u.length = v.length → u = v
'Heraclitus.ReturnPrefix.valid_words_unique' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase
Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (w pre : List Int) (d : Int)
  (markers : Int → Prop) (q : Nat),
  Heraclitus.ValidWord s w →
    Heraclitus.execute s w = Heraclitus.translate d s →
      Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark s markers)
          (Heraclitus.repeatedWord w d q ++ List.map (fun x => x + ↑q * d) pre) =
        Heraclitus.mark (Heraclitus.translate (↑q * d) (Heraclitus.execute s pre)) markers
'Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase_components
Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase_components : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (w pre : List Int) (d : Int)
  (markers : Int → Prop) (q : Nat),
  Heraclitus.ValidWord s w →
    Heraclitus.execute s w = Heraclitus.translate d s →
      have st :=
        Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark s markers)
          (Heraclitus.repeatedWord w d q ++ List.map (fun x => x + ↑q * d) pre);
      st.current = (Heraclitus.execute s pre).current + ↑q * d ∧
        ∀ (x : Int), st.used x ↔ (Heraclitus.execute s pre).used (x - ↑q * d) ∨ markers x
'Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase_components' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase_valid
Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase_valid : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (w pre : List Int) (d : Int)
  (markers : Int → Prop) (q : Nat),
  Heraclitus.ValidWord s w →
    Heraclitus.execute s w = Heraclitus.translate d s →
      Heraclitus.ValidWord s pre →
        (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ Heraclitus.repeatedWord w d q ++ List.map (fun x => x + ↑q * d) pre → ¬markers x) →
          Heraclitus.ValidWord (Heraclitus.mark s markers)
            (Heraclitus.repeatedWord w d q ++ List.map (fun x => x + ↑q * d) pre)
'Heraclitus.ReturnTailCycle.marked_cycle_phase_valid' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.RoutePrefix.actual_signed_route_prefix
Heraclitus.RoutePrefix.actual_signed_route_prefix : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (base : Nat) (r : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.Route) (f C : Int) (i : Nat) (markers : Int → Prop) (side : Bool)
      (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
          Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
            (Heraclitus.mark (Heraclitus.translate f r.initial.state) markers) →
        Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base)
            (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) (r.word f)) →
          i ≤ (r.word f).length →
            (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ List.take i (r.word f) → 0 < C + x) →
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + i) =
                Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                  (Heraclitus.mark
                    (Heraclitus.translate (f + Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.advance (List.take i r.edges))
                      (Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.last r.initial (List.take i r.edges)).state)
                    markers)
'Heraclitus.RoutePrefix.actual_signed_route_prefix' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

  Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.translate f r.initial.state) (List.take i (r.word f)) =
    Heraclitus.translate (f + Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.advance (List.take i r.edges))
      (Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.last r.initial (List.take i r.edges)).state

BEGIN Heraclitus.ShortTail43.certified_short_prefix_cover
Heraclitus.ShortTail43.certified_short_prefix_cover : ∀ (k : Nat),
  100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
    Heraclitus.ValidWord (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } (↑(7 ^ k)) 2).toState [2, 1] ∧
      ∀ (i : Nat),
        i ≤ 2 →
          ∀ (x : Int),
            have st :=
              Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } (↑(7 ^ k)) 2).toState
                (List.take i [2, 1]);
            st.current < x → st.used x → x = ↑(7 ^ k) + 2 ∨ x = 2 * ↑(7 ^ k) + 2 ∨ x = 3 * ↑(7 ^ k) + 2 ∨ x = 2
'Heraclitus.ShortTail43.certified_short_prefix_cover' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

  0 < C + x → ((Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other s).used (Heraclitus.embedRay side C x) ↔ s.used x)

BEGIN Heraclitus.ShortTail43.prefix_ahead_count

  1 ≤ m →
    ∀ (i : Nat),
      i ≤ 2 →
        ∀ (xs : List Int),
          xs.Nodup →
            (∀ (x : Int),
                x ∈ xs →
                  have st :=
                    Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState
                      (List.take i [2, 1]);
                  st.current < x ∧ st.used x) →
              xs.length ≤ 4

BEGIN Heraclitus.SignedTail43.actual_phase43_short_tail
Heraclitus.SignedTail43.actual_phase43_short_tail : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (t : Nat),
      ∃ q base,
        7 ^ (7 + 36 * t) ≤ base ∧
          Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
              Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!Heraclitus.ShortReturnData.phase.entrance.outgoing.row.positive)
                (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (7 + 36 * t))
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary Heraclitus.ShortReturnData.phase.entrance.outgoing q)
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } (↑(7 ^ (7 + 36 * t))) 2).toState ∧
            ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ 2 → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 4
'Heraclitus.SignedTail43.actual_phase43_short_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.SignedTail43.actual_walk_tail_count
Heraclitus.SignedTail43.actual_walk_tail_count : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (base k : Nat) (side : Bool) (C : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
      2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
        100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
          3 * ↑(7 ^ k) - 200 ≤ other.front →
            Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                  (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } (↑(7 ^ k)) 2).toState →
              ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ 2 → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 4

END

  0 ≤ C →
    ∀ (i : Nat),
      i ≤ 2 →
        Heraclitus.execute
            (Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
              (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState)

          Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other

              (List.take i [2, 1]))

    ∀ (t : Nat),
      ∃ q base,
        7 ^ (7 + 36 * t) ≤ base ∧

              Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!Heraclitus.ShortReturnData.phase.entrance.outgoing.row.positive)
                (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (7 + 36 * t))
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary Heraclitus.ShortReturnData.phase.entrance.outgoing q)

            ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ 2 → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 4

BEGIN Heraclitus.SignedTail43.physical_prefix_tail_count
Heraclitus.SignedTail43.physical_prefix_tail_count : ∀ (side : Bool) (C m : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
  0 ≤ C →
    1 ≤ m →
      ∀ (i : Nat),
        i ≤ 2 →
          ∀ (xs : List Int),
            xs.Nodup →
              (∀ (z : Int),
                  z ∈ xs →
                    have physical :=
                      Heraclitus.execute
                        (Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                          (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState)
                        (List.take i (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) [2, 1]));
                    0 < z ∧ ↑physical.current.natAbs < z ∧ physical.used (Heraclitus.ray side z)) →
                xs.length ≤ 4
'Heraclitus.SignedTail43.physical_prefix_tail_count' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.SignedTail43.physical_prefix_tail_cover
Heraclitus.SignedTail43.physical_prefix_tail_cover : ∀ (side : Bool) (C m : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
  0 ≤ C →
    1 ≤ m →
      ∀ (i : Nat),
        i ≤ 2 →
          ∀ (z : Int),
            0 < z →
              ↑(Heraclitus.execute
                          (Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                            (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState)
                          (List.take i (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) [2, 1]))).current.natAbs <
                  z →
                (Heraclitus.execute
                        (Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                          (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState)
                        (List.take i (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) [2, 1]))).used
                    (Heraclitus.ray side z) →
                  z ∈ [C + m + 2, C + 2 * m + 2, C + 3 * m + 2, C + 2]
'Heraclitus.SignedTail43.physical_prefix_tail_cover' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.SignedTail43.signed_prefix_radius
Heraclitus.SignedTail43.signed_prefix_radius : ∀ (side : Bool) (C m : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
  0 ≤ C →
    1 ≤ m →
      ∀ (i : Nat),
        i ≤ 2 →
          have half :=
            Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState
              (List.take i [2, 1]);
          have physical :=
            Heraclitus.execute
              (Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState)
              (List.take i (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) [2, 1]));
          ↑physical.current.natAbs = C + half.current
'Heraclitus.SignedTail43.signed_prefix_radius' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.SignedTail43.signed_prefix_tail_count

  0 ≤ C →
    1 ≤ m →
      ∀ (i : Nat),
        i ≤ 2 →
          ∀ (xs : List Int),
            xs.Nodup →
              (∀ (z : Int),
                  z ∈ xs →
                    have half :=
                      Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState
                        (List.take i [2, 1]);

                          (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint { front := 1, extra := [] } m 2).toState)
                        (List.take i (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) [2, 1]));
                    0 < z ∧ C + half.current < z ∧ physical.used (Heraclitus.embedRay side C (z - C))) →
                xs.length ≤ 4

BEGIN Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead_length

    (-c - 1).toNat + (List.filter (fun b => Decidable.decide (c < b)) B).length

BEGIN Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead_mem

  c < x → x < 0 ∨ x ∈ B → x ∈ Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead c B

BEGIN Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_state_tail_53
Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_state_tail_53 : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (F c h shift outer₁ outer₂ : Int) (levels : Nat)
  (B xs : List Int),
  xs.Nodup →
    s.current = F + c →
      (∀ (x : Int),
          s.used x →
            x < F ∨
              x - F ∈ B ∨ Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower h levels (x - F - shift) ∨ x - F = outer₁ ∨ x - F = outer₂) →
        (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ xs → s.current < x ∧ s.used x) →
          (Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead c B).length ≤ 53 → xs.length ≤ 55 + 6 * levels
'Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_state_tail_53' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_state_tail_cover
Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_state_tail_cover : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (F c h shift outer₁ outer₂ : Int)
  (levels : Nat) (B xs : List Int),
  xs.Nodup →
    s.current = F + c →
      (∀ (x : Int),
          s.used x →
            x < F ∨
              x - F ∈ B ∨ Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower h levels (x - F - shift) ∨ x - F = outer₁ ∨ x - F = outer₂) →
        (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ xs → s.current < x ∧ s.used x) →
          xs.length ≤ (Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead c B).length + 6 * levels + 2
'Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_state_tail_cover' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_tail_cover
Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_tail_cover : ∀ (c h shift outer₁ outer₂ : Int) (levels : Nat) (B xs : List Int),
  xs.Nodup →
    (∀ (x : Int),
        x ∈ xs →
          c < x ∧ (x < 0 ∨ x ∈ B ∨ Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower h levels (x - shift) ∨ x = outer₁ ∨ x = outer₂)) →
      xs.length ≤ (Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead c B).length + 6 * levels + 2
'Heraclitus.TailCover.physical_tail_cover' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.Transfer.base_power_translation

  Heraclitus.Transfer.BasePowerStep base (x + d) (y + d) ↔ Heraclitus.Transfer.BasePowerStep base x y

END
BEGIN Heraclitus.Transfer.cycle_extrema
Heraclitus.Transfer.cycle_extrema : ∀ (n radius shift total : Int),
  0 ≤ shift →
    shift ≤ total →
      Heraclitus.Transfer.outward (n + shift) (radius + shift) ≤
          Heraclitus.Transfer.outward (n + total) (radius + total) ∧
        Heraclitus.Transfer.inward (n + shift) (radius + shift) ≤ Heraclitus.Transfer.inward n radius
'Heraclitus.Transfer.cycle_extrema' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.Transfer.defect_bound_iff
Heraclitus.Transfer.defect_bound_iff : ∀ (n radius K : Int),
  4 * Heraclitus.Transfer.twiceDeviation n radius - 3 * n ≤ K ↔
    Heraclitus.Transfer.outward n radius ≤ K ∧ Heraclitus.Transfer.inward n radius ≤ K
'Heraclitus.Transfer.defect_bound_iff' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.Transfer.inward_cycle_increment
Heraclitus.Transfer.inward_cycle_increment : ∀ (n radius shift : Int),
  Heraclitus.Transfer.inward (n + shift) (radius + shift) = Heraclitus.Transfer.inward n radius - 7 * shift
'Heraclitus.Transfer.inward_cycle_increment' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.Transfer.outward_cycle_increment
Heraclitus.Transfer.outward_cycle_increment : ∀ (n radius shift : Int),
  Heraclitus.Transfer.outward (n + shift) (radius + shift) = Heraclitus.Transfer.outward n radius + shift
'Heraclitus.Transfer.outward_cycle_increment' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.Transfer.phase_only_obstruction
Heraclitus.Transfer.phase_only_obstruction : ∀ (n radius penalty P : Int),
  0 < P → Heraclitus.Transfer.outward n radius + penalty < Heraclitus.Transfer.outward (n + P) (radius + P) + penalty
'Heraclitus.Transfer.phase_only_obstruction' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.Transfer.return_envelope
Heraclitus.Transfer.return_envelope : ∀ (m C D n radius H B delta : Int),
  2 * C = m - 1 ∧ 2 * D = 7 * m - 1 →
    4 * m + delta ≤ n →
      radius ≤ D + H →
        C - H ≤ radius →
          n ≤ D + radius + B →
            Heraclitus.Transfer.outward n radius ≤ 8 * H - 4 - 7 * delta ∧
              Heraclitus.Transfer.inward n radius ≤ 7 * H + 3 + B
'Heraclitus.Transfer.return_envelope' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TransferCounting.Checkpoint.exact_scale
Heraclitus.TransferCounting.Checkpoint.exact_scale : ∀ (r : Heraclitus.TransferCounting.Checkpoint),
  r.Claim →
    ∀ (k : Nat) (C : Int),
      2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
        100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
          ∀ (w : List Int),
            Heraclitus.SignedValidWord Heraclitus.signedStart w →
              Heraclitus.execute Heraclitus.signedStart w =
                  Heraclitus.signedBoundary C r.pos r.neg r.positive r.current →
                w.length = 7 ^ k
'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.Checkpoint.exact_scale' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TransferCounting.exact_index
Heraclitus.TransferCounting.exact_index : ∀ (w : List Int),
  Heraclitus.SignedValidWord Heraclitus.signedStart w →
    ∀ (C : Int) (pos neg : Heraclitus.RayBoundary) (side : Bool) (current : Int),
      Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C pos →
        Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C neg →
          Heraclitus.execute Heraclitus.signedStart w = Heraclitus.signedBoundary C pos neg side current →
            ↑w.length = 2 * C + pos.front + neg.front - 2 + ↑pos.extra.length + ↑neg.extra.length
'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.exact_index' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_length
Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_length : ∀ (C : Int) (pos neg : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
  Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C pos →
    Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C neg →
      ↑(Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history C pos neg).length =
        2 * C + pos.front + neg.front - 1 + ↑pos.extra.length + ↑neg.extra.length
'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_length' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_mem
Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_mem : ∀ (C : Int) (pos neg : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
  Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C pos →
    Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C neg →
      ∀ (side : Bool) (current z : Int),
        z ∈ Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history C pos neg ↔ (Heraclitus.signedBoundary C pos neg side current).used z
'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_mem' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_nodup
Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_nodup : ∀ (C : Int) (pos neg : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
  Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C pos →
    Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C neg → (Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history C pos neg).Nodup
'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.history_nodup' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TransferCounting.positivePoints_mem
Heraclitus.TransferCounting.positivePoints_mem : ∀ (C : Int) (b : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
  Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C b →
    ∀ (z : Int), z ∈ Heraclitus.TransferCounting.positivePoints C b ↔ 0 < z ∧ Heraclitus.boundaryUsed b (z - C)
'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.positivePoints_mem' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END

'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.positivePoints_nodup' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.TransferCounting.weight_three_index
Heraclitus.TransferCounting.weight_three_index : ∀ (w : List Int),
  Heraclitus.SignedValidWord Heraclitus.signedStart w →
    ∀ (C : Int) (pos neg : Heraclitus.RayBoundary) (side : Bool) (current : Int),
      Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C pos →
        Heraclitus.TransferCounting.WellFormed C neg →
          Heraclitus.execute Heraclitus.signedStart w = Heraclitus.signedBoundary C pos neg side current →
            pos.front + neg.front + ↑pos.extra.length + ↑neg.extra.length = 3 → ↑w.length = 2 * C + 1
'Heraclitus.TransferCounting.weight_three_index' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.eventual_upper_of_proposed_envelope
Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.eventual_upper_of_proposed_envelope : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  (∀ (k : Nat),
      6 ≤ k →
        ∀ (n : Nat), 7 ^ k ≤ n → n < 7 ^ (k + 1) → Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a n ≤ 32771 + 6 * (↑k - 4)) →
    Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.EventualUpperBound a
'Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.eventual_upper_of_proposed_envelope' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.eventual_upper_of_scale_bound
Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.eventual_upper_of_scale_bound : ∀ (a : Nat → Int) (A B : Nat),
  (∀ (k : Nat),
      6 ≤ k → ∀ (n : Nat), 7 ^ k ≤ n → n < 7 ^ (k + 1) → Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a n ≤ ↑(A + B * k)) →
    Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.EventualUpperBound a
'Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.eventual_upper_of_scale_bound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.scale_bracket
Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.scale_bracket : ∀ (n : Nat), 0 < n → ∃ k, 7 ^ k ≤ n ∧ n < 7 ^ (k + 1)
'Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.scale_bracket' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.square_le_pow
Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.square_le_pow : ∀ (k : Nat), k * k ≤ 7 ^ k
'Heraclitus.UpperAsymptotic.square_le_pow' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_boundary_tail
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_boundary_tail : ∀ (b : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.CheckedBoundary) (K : Nat),
  Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.AllGood K b.initial b.moves →
    ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
        ∀ (base k : Nat) (C : Int),
          2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
            100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base = b.initial.toState C →
                ∀ (D i : Nat), i ≤ b.moves.length → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ K
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_boundary_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_catalogue_boundary
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_catalogue_boundary : ∀ (b : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.CheckedBoundary),
  b ∈ Heraclitus.SignedBoundaryData.catalogue →
    ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
        ∀ (base k : Nat) (C : Int),
          2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
            100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base = b.initial.toState C →
                Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base)
                    (Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.physicalWord C b.moves) ∧
                  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + b.moves.length) = b.final.toState C ∧
                    ∀ (D i : Nat), i ≤ b.moves.length → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 39
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_catalogue_boundary' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_catalogue_boundary_tail
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_catalogue_boundary_tail : ∀ (b : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.CheckedBoundary),
  b ∈ Heraclitus.SignedBoundaryData.catalogue →
    ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
        ∀ (base k : Nat) (C : Int),
          2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
            100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base = b.initial.toState C →
                ∀ (D i : Nat), i ≤ b.moves.length → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 39
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_catalogue_boundary_tail' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_prefix_state
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_prefix_state : ∀ (b : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.CheckedBoundary)
  (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (base k : Nat) (C : Int),
      2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
        100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
          Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base = b.initial.toState C →
            ∀ (i : Nat),
              i ≤ b.moves.length →
                Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + i) = (b.initial.run (List.take i b.moves)).toState C
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_prefix_state' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_record_tail
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_record_tail : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (n D : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Record) (C : Int) (K : Nat),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a n = s.toState C →
        0 < C + s.current → Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.score s ≤ K → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a n D ≤ K
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.actual_record_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.allGood_prefix
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.allGood_prefix : ∀ (K : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Record)
  (ms : List Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Move),
  Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.AllGood K s ms →
    ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ ms.length → Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.Good K (s.run (List.take i ms))
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.allGood_prefix' does not depend on any axioms
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.all_boundaries
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.all_boundaries : ∀ (b : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.CheckedBoundary),
  b ∈ Heraclitus.SignedBoundaryData.catalogue → Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.AllGood 39 b.initial b.moves
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.all_boundaries' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.inner_membership
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.inner_membership : ∀ (i : Fin 36),
  (Heraclitus.ScaleScheduleData.entry i).checked.link.phase.entrance.outgoing.inner ∈
    Heraclitus.SignedBoundaryData.catalogue
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.inner_membership' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.original_rule_inner_tail
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.original_rule_inner_tail : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      6 ≤ k →
        ∀ (D i : Nat),
          i ≤ (Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.scheduledInner k).moves.length →
            Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (7 ^ k + i) D ≤ 39
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.original_rule_inner_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.record_cover
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.record_cover : ∀ (K : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Record),
  Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.score s ≤ K →
    Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.Covered K (fun x => False)
      (Heraclitus.halfBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.active s) s.current)
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.record_cover' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.record_lift
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.record_lift : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Record) (C : Int),
  s.toState C =
    Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay s.positive C (s.ray !s.positive)
      (Heraclitus.halfBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.active s) s.current)
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.record_lift' does not depend on any axioms
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.scheduled_inner_start
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.scheduled_inner_start : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      6 ≤ k →
        Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (7 ^ k) =
          (Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.scheduledInner k).initial.toState (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k)
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.scheduled_inner_start' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.wordCheck_take
Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.wordCheck_take : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Record)
  (ms : List Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Move),
  Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.wordCheck s ms = Bool.true →
    ∀ (i : Nat), Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.wordCheck s (List.take i ms) = Bool.true
'Heraclitus.UpperBoundaryPrefix.wordCheck_take' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.allLocal_append
Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.allLocal_append : ∀ (K : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.State) (u v : List Int),
  Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K s u →
    Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K (Heraclitus.execute s u) v →
      Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K s (u ++ v)
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.allLocal_append' does not depend on any axioms
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.allLocal_prefix
Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.allLocal_prefix : ∀ (K : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int),
  Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K s w →
    ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ w.length → Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.HasLocal K (Heraclitus.execute s (List.take i w))
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.allLocal_prefix' does not depend on any axioms
END

  c ∈ Heraclitus.LadderBottomData.bottom17.chunks →
    Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal 53 c.chunk.initial.toState c.chunk.word
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.bottom17_chunks' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.bottom_allLocal
Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.bottom_allLocal : ∀ (K : Nat) (b : Heraclitus.LadderBottom.CheckedBottom),
  (Heraclitus.TailCover.localAhead (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.normalized b.bottom.output).current
          (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.normalized b.bottom.output).points).length ≤
      K →
    (∀ (c : Heraclitus.LadderBottom.CheckedChunk),
        c ∈ b.bottom.chunks → Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K c.chunk.initial.toState c.chunk.word) →
      Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K (Heraclitus.LadderDescent.start b.bottom.entrance.entrance.old 2401)
        b.bottom.word
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.bottom_allLocal' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.matches_sound
Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.matches_sound : ∀ (K : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.LadderBottom.Bitmap)
  (f : Heraclitus.FiniteState),
  Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.Matches K s f → Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.HasLocal K s.toState
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.matches_sound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.route_allLocal
Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.route_allLocal : ∀ (K : Nat) (s out : Heraclitus.LadderBottom.Bitmap)
  (cs : List Heraclitus.LadderBottom.CheckedChunk),
  Heraclitus.LadderBottom.Linked s cs out →
    Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.HasLocal K out.toState →
      (∀ (c : Heraclitus.LadderBottom.CheckedChunk),
          c ∈ cs → Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K c.chunk.initial.toState c.chunk.word) →
        Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K s.toState (Heraclitus.LadderBottom.routeWord cs)
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.route_allLocal' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.signed_marker_count
Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.signed_marker_count : Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.signedMarkerRows.length = 8
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.signed_marker_count' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

  c ∈ Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.signedMarkerRows →
    Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound 43 (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transferNodes c.transfer)
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.signed_markers_bound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transfer000_bound

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transfer_route_bounds
Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transfer_route_bounds : ∀ (t : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.Transfer) (K : Nat),
  Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound K (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transferNodes t) →
    Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound K (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.routeNodes t.transient) ∧
      Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound K (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.routeNodes t.cycle) ∧
        Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound K (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.routeNodes t.before) ∧
          Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound K (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.routeNodes t.repair)
'Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transfer_route_bounds' depends on axioms: [propext]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_finite_tail
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_finite_tail : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (n D : Nat) (side : Bool) (C : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary) (s : Heraclitus.State),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a n = Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other s →
        0 < C + s.current →
          ∀ (budget : Nat),
            Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.FiniteCover budget s → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a n D ≤ budget
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_finite_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_ladder_tail
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_ladder_tail : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.LadderEnvelope.Checked),
  c ∈ Heraclitus.LadderEnvelopeData.catalogue →
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      2 ≤ n →
        ∀ (side : Bool) (C : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
          2 * C + 1 = Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n →
            3 * Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n - 200 ≤ other.front →
              ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
                Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
                  ∀ (base D : Nat),
                    Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                        Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other (c.initial n).toState →
                      ∃ w,
                        Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w ∧
                          Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w =
                              Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other (c.output n) ∧
                            ↑w.length = c.moves n ∧
                              ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ w.length → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 55 + 6 * n
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_ladder_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_prefix_bound
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_prefix_bound : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (base D : Nat) (side : Bool) (C : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary) (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base = Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other s →
        Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) w) →
          0 < C + s.current →
            (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ w → 0 < C + x) →
              ∀ (budget : Nat),
                Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget s w →
                  ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ w.length → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ budget
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.actual_prefix_bound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.allLocal_cover
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.allLocal_cover : ∀ (K : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int),
  Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal K s w → Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K (fun x => False) s w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.allLocal_cover' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_append
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_append : ∀ (budget : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.State) (u v : List Int),
  Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget s u →
    Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget (Heraclitus.execute s u) v →
      Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget s (u ++ v)
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_append' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_cover
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_cover : ∀ (K : Nat) (M : Int → Prop) (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int),
  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K M s w →
    ∀ (markers : List Int),
      (∀ (x : Int), M x → x ∈ markers) →
        ∀ (budget : Nat), K + markers.length ≤ budget → Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget s w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_cover' does not depend on any axioms
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_mark
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_mark : ∀ (budget : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int) (N : Int → Prop)
  (marks : List Int),
  (∀ (x : Int), N x → x ∈ marks) →
    Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget s w →
      Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite (budget + marks.length) (Heraclitus.mark s N) w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_mark' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_translate
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_translate : ∀ (budget : Nat) (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int) (d : Int),
  Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget s w →
    Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite budget (Heraclitus.translate d s) (List.map (fun x => x + d) w)
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_finite_translate' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_ladders

  c ∈ Heraclitus.LadderLayersData.catalogue → Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.LadderBound 53 c.ladder

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_mark
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_mark : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int) (K : Nat) (M N : Int → Prop),
  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K M s w →
    Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K (fun x => M x ∨ N x) (Heraclitus.mark s N) w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_mark' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_translate
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_translate : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int) (d : Int) (K : Nat)
  (M N : Int → Prop),
  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K M s w →
    (∀ (x : Int), M (x - d) → N x) →
      Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K N (Heraclitus.translate d s) (List.map (fun x => x + d) w)
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.all_translate' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.ascent_prefix
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.ascent_prefix : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.LadderLayers.CheckedLadder),
  c ∈ Heraclitus.LadderLayersData.catalogue →
    ∀ (n levels : Nat),
      ∃ w,
        Heraclitus.ValidWord
            (Heraclitus.mark (c.ladder.entry n).toState
              (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) levels))
            w ∧
          Heraclitus.execute
                (Heraclitus.mark (c.ladder.entry n).toState
                  (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) levels))
                w =
              Heraclitus.translate (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale (n + levels) - Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n)
                (c.ladder.entry (n + levels)).toState ∧
            ↑w.length =
                Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale (n + levels) - Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n +
                  c.ladder.cumulative n levels ∧
              Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite (53 + 6 * levels)
                (Heraclitus.mark (c.ladder.entry n).toState
                  (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) levels))
                w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.ascent_prefix' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.bottom_ladder_bound
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.bottom_ladder_bound : ∀ (b : Heraclitus.LadderBottom.CheckedBottom),
  b ∈ Heraclitus.LadderBottomData.catalogue → Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.LadderBound 53 b.bottom.ladder.ladder
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.bottom_ladder_bound' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.chain
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.chain : ∀ (s out : Heraclitus.FiniteState)
  (cs : List Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.CheckedTransfer),
  Heraclitus.LadderLayers.Linked s cs out →
    ∀ (h : Int),
      2401 ≤ h →
        (∀ (c : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.CheckedTransfer),
            c ∈ cs → h % ↑c.transfer.modulus = c.transfer.residue) →
          ∀ (far : Int → Prop),
            (∀ (x : Int), far x → (↑cs.length + 1) * h ≤ x) →
              ∀ (K : Nat),
                Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.FiniteBound K s →
                  (∀ (c : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.CheckedTransfer),
                      c ∈ cs →
                        Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound K
                          (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transferNodes c.transfer)) →
                    ∃ w,
                      Heraclitus.ValidWord (Heraclitus.mark s.toState (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending h cs.length far))
                          w ∧
                        Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark s.toState (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending h cs.length far))
                              w =
                            Heraclitus.mark (Heraclitus.translate (↑cs.length * h) out.toState) far ∧
                          ↑w.length = ↑cs.length * h + Heraclitus.LadderLayers.corrections cs ∧
                            Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K
                              (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending h cs.length far)
                              (Heraclitus.mark s.toState (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending h cs.length far)) w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.chain' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.complete_prefix
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.complete_prefix : ∀ (b : Heraclitus.LadderBottom.CheckedBottom),
  b ∈ Heraclitus.LadderBottomData.catalogue →
    Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.AllLocal 53 (Heraclitus.LadderDescent.start b.bottom.entrance.entrance.old 2401)
        b.bottom.word →
      ∀ (n : Nat),
        ∃ w,
          Heraclitus.ValidWord
              (Heraclitus.LadderDescent.start b.bottom.entrance.entrance.old (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n)) w ∧
            Heraclitus.execute
                  (Heraclitus.LadderDescent.start b.bottom.entrance.entrance.old (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n)) w =
                Heraclitus.translate (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) (b.bottom.ladder.ladder.entry n).toState ∧
              ↑w.length =
                  6 * ↑n + ↑b.bottom.moves + Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n - 2401 +
                    b.bottom.ladder.ladder.cumulative 0 n ∧
                Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite (53 + 6 * n)
                  (Heraclitus.LadderDescent.start b.bottom.entrance.entrance.old (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n)) w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.complete_prefix' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.covered_mark
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.covered_mark : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (K : Nat) (M N : Int → Prop),
  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.Covered K M s →
    Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.Covered K (fun x => M x ∨ N x) (Heraclitus.mark s N)
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.covered_mark' does not depend on any axioms
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.covered_translate
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.covered_translate : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (d : Int) (K : Nat) (M N : Int → Prop),
  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.Covered K M s →
    (∀ (x : Int), M (x - d) → N x) → Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.Covered K N (Heraclitus.translate d s)
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.covered_translate' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.current_lower
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.current_lower : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.State) (w : List Int) (lo : Int),
  lo ≤ s.current → (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ w → lo ≤ x) → lo ≤ (Heraclitus.execute s w).current
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.current_lower' depends on axioms: [propext]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.descent_prefix
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.descent_prefix : ∀ (old : Int → Prop),
  Heraclitus.LadderDescent.Region old →
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite (53 + 6 * n)
        (Heraclitus.LadderDescent.start old (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n)) (Heraclitus.LadderDescent.word 4 n)
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.descent_prefix' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.finite_covered
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.finite_covered : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.FiniteState) (K : Nat),
  Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.FiniteBound K s →
    ∀ (M : Int → Prop), Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.Covered K M (Heraclitus.mark s.toState M)
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.finite_covered' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.halfline_all_prefixes
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.halfline_all_prefixes : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.LadderEnvelope.Checked),
  c ∈ Heraclitus.LadderEnvelopeData.catalogue →
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      ∃ w,
        Heraclitus.ValidWord (c.initial n).toState w ∧
          Heraclitus.execute (c.initial n).toState w = c.output n ∧
            ↑w.length = c.moves n ∧
              Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.Envelope (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) (c.initial n).toState w ∧
                (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ w → -100 ≤ x) ∧
                  Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.AllFinite (55 + 6 * n) (c.initial n).toState w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.halfline_all_prefixes' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.normalized_state
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.normalized_state : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.FiniteState),
  s.toState = Heraclitus.translate s.front (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.normalized s).state
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.normalized_state' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.one_layer
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.one_layer : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.LadderLayers.Ladder),
  c.Claim →
    ∀ (K : Nat),
      Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.LadderBound K c →
        ∀ (n : Nat) (far : Int → Prop),
          (∀ (x : Int), far x → 7 * Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n ≤ x) →
            ∃ w,
              Heraclitus.ValidWord
                  (Heraclitus.mark (c.entry n).toState
                    (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) 6 far))
                  w ∧
                Heraclitus.execute
                      (Heraclitus.mark (c.entry n).toState
                        (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) 6 far))
                      w =
                    Heraclitus.mark
                      (Heraclitus.translate (6 * Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) (c.entry (n + 1)).toState) far ∧
                  ↑w.length = 6 * Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n + c.layerCorrection n ∧
                    Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K
                      (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) 6 far)
                      (Heraclitus.mark (c.entry n).toState
                        (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.pending (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) 6 far))
                      w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.one_layer' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.tower
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.tower : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.LadderLayers.Ladder),
  c.Claim →
    ∀ (K : Nat),
      Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.LadderBound K c →
        ∀ (n levels : Nat),
          ∃ w,
            Heraclitus.ValidWord
                (Heraclitus.mark (c.entry n).toState
                  (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) levels))
                w ∧
              Heraclitus.execute
                    (Heraclitus.mark (c.entry n).toState
                      (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) levels))
                    w =
                  Heraclitus.translate (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale (n + levels) - Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n)
                    (c.entry (n + levels)).toState ∧
                ↑w.length =
                    Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale (n + levels) - Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n +
                      c.cumulative n levels ∧
                  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K
                    (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) levels)
                    (Heraclitus.mark (c.entry n).toState
                      (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower (Heraclitus.LadderLayers.scale n) levels))
                    w
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.tower' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.transfer_output_bound
Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.transfer_output_bound : ∀ (t : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.Transfer) (K : Nat),
  Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.nodesBound K (Heraclitus.UpperKernelCatalogue.transferNodes t) →
    Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.FiniteBound K t.output
'Heraclitus.UpperLadderPrefix.transfer_output_bound' depends on axioms: [propext]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.actual_cycle_frame
Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.actual_cycle_frame : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.CertifiedRow) (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (k : Nat) (C d : Int),
      2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
        100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
          Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (7 ^ k) = c.inner.initial.toState C →
            ↑(7 ^ k) = c.row.scale + c.row.model.period * d →
              ∀ (q u : Nat),
                ↑q = c.row.winningCycle + 3 * d →
                  u ≤ q →
                    ∀ (v : Fin c.row.phases.size),
                      (u = q → ↑v ≤ ↑c.row.selected) →
                        Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (7 ^ k + c.inner.moves.length + u * c.row.phases.size + ↑v) =
                          c.cycle.frame c.geometry C u ↑v
'Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.actual_cycle_frame' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.all_row_bounds
Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.all_row_bounds : ∀ (i : Fin 36),
  Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.RowBounds (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing
'Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.all_row_bounds' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.boundary_prefix_band
Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.boundary_prefix_band : ∀ (s : Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Record)
  (ms : List Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.Move),
  -512 ≤ s.current ∧ s.current ≤ 512 →
    Heraclitus.SignedCertificate.wordCheck s ms = Bool.true →
      ∀ (j : Nat), j ≤ ms.length → -512 ≤ (s.run (List.take j ms)).current ∧ (s.run (List.take j ms)).current ≤ 512
'Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.boundary_prefix_band' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.cycle_defect
Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.cycle_defect : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.CertifiedRow),
  Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.RowBounds c →
    ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
        ∀ (k : Nat) (C d : Int),
          2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
            100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (7 ^ k) = c.inner.initial.toState C →
                ↑(7 ^ k) = c.row.scale + c.row.model.period * d →
                  ∀ (q u : Nat),
                    ↑q = c.row.winningCycle + 3 * d →
                      u ≤ q →
                        ∀ (v : Fin c.row.phases.size),
                          (u = q → ↑v ≤ ↑c.row.selected) →
                            Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a
                                (7 ^ k + c.inner.moves.length + u * c.row.phases.size + ↑v) ≤
                              16261
'Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.cycle_defect' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.defect_of_two_bounds
Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.defect_of_two_bounds : ∀ (a : Nat → Int) (n : Nat) (K : Int),
  8 * ↑(a n).natAbs - 7 * ↑n ≤ K → ↑n - 8 * ↑(a n).natAbs ≤ K → Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a n ≤ K
'Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.defect_of_two_bounds' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.inner_defect
Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.inner_defect : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.CertifiedRow),
  Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.RowBounds c →
    ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
        ∀ (k : Nat) (C : Int),
          2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
            100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (7 ^ k) = c.inner.initial.toState C →
                ∀ (j : Nat), j ≤ c.inner.moves.length → Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a (7 ^ k + j) ≤ 16261
'Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.inner_defect' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.original_rule_before_crossing
Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.original_rule_before_crossing : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36) (t q : Nat),
      (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).CycleIndex t q →
        ∀ (n : Nat),
          7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t ≤ n →
            n < Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.crossingIndex i t q → Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a n ≤ 16261
'Heraclitus.UpperOutgoingPrefix.original_rule_before_crossing' depends on axioms: [propext,
 Classical.choice,
 Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder_member
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder_member : ∀ (i : Fin 36),
  Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i ∈ Heraclitus.LadderEnvelopeData.catalogue
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder_member' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_all_phase_routes
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_all_phase_routes : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36) (t : Nat),
      have p := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i;
      have k := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t;
      have C := Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k;
      ∃ q base w,
        base =
            7 ^ k +
              (p.entrance.outgoing.inner.moves.length + q * p.entrance.outgoing.row.phases.size +
                  ↑p.entrance.outgoing.row.selected +
                3) ∧
          Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
              Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q)
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint p.entrance.outgoing.geometry.other (↑(7 ^ k))
                    p.entrance.data.anchor).toState ∧
            p.CycleIndex t q ∧
              Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w ∧
                Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + w.length) =
                    Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                      (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q) (p.endpoint t) ∧
                  ↑w.length = p.route.moves (k - 4) ∧
                    ∀ (j : Nat),
                      j ≤ w.length → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + j) D ≤ 55 + 6 * (k - 4)
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_all_phase_routes' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_all_scales
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_all_scales : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (k : Nat),
      6 ≤ k →
        have i := Heraclitus.ScaleSchedule.index (k - 6);
        have t := (k - 6) / 36;
        have p := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i;
        have C := Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k;
        ∃ q base w,
          base =
              7 ^ k +
                (p.entrance.outgoing.inner.moves.length + q * p.entrance.outgoing.row.phases.size +
                    ↑p.entrance.outgoing.row.selected +
                  3) ∧
            Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                  (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q)
                  (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint p.entrance.outgoing.geometry.other (↑(7 ^ k))
                      p.entrance.data.anchor).toState ∧
              p.CycleIndex t q ∧
                Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w ∧
                  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + w.length) =
                      Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                        (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q) (p.endpoint t) ∧
                    ↑w.length = p.route.moves (k - 4) ∧
                      ∀ (j : Nat),
                        j ≤ w.length → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + j) D ≤ 55 + 6 * (k - 4)
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_all_scales' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_ordinary_ladder
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_ordinary_ladder : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36),
      ↑i ≠ 1 →
        ∀ (t : Nat),
          have p := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i;
          have k := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t;
          have C := Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k;
          ∃ q base w,
            base =
                7 ^ k +
                  (p.entrance.outgoing.inner.moves.length + q * p.entrance.outgoing.row.phases.size +
                      ↑p.entrance.outgoing.row.selected +
                    3) ∧
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                  Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                    (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q)
                    ((Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i).initial (k - 4)).toState ∧
                p.CycleIndex t q ∧
                  Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w ∧
                    Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + w.length) =
                        Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                          (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q)
                          ((Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i).output (k - 4)) ∧
                      ↑w.length = (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i).moves (k - 4) ∧
                        ∀ (j : Nat),
                          j ≤ w.length → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + j) D ≤ 55 + 6 * (k - 4)
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.original_rule_ordinary_ladder' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_checkpoint
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_checkpoint : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36) (t : Nat),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t) =
        (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing.inner.initial.toState
          (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t))
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_checkpoint' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_match
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_match : ∀ (i : Fin 36),
  ↑i ≠ 1 →
    Heraclitus.SignedBottom.Match (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance
      (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i).original
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_match' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_route
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_route : ∀ (i : Fin 36) (ordinary : ↑i ≠ 1),
  (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).route =
    Heraclitus.SignedLadder.Route.ladder (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i) ⋯
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase_route' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.reached_entrance
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.reached_entrance : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36) (t : Nat),
      have p := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i;
      have k := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t;
      have C := Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k;
      ∃ q base,
        base =
            7 ^ k +
              (p.entrance.outgoing.inner.moves.length + q * p.entrance.outgoing.row.phases.size +
                  ↑p.entrance.outgoing.row.selected +
                3) ∧
          Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
              Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q)
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint p.entrance.outgoing.geometry.other (↑(7 ^ k))
                    p.entrance.data.anchor).toState ∧
            3 * ↑(7 ^ k) - 200 ≤ (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q).front ∧ p.CycleIndex t q
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.reached_entrance' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.reached_ordinary_ladder
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.reached_ordinary_ladder : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36),
      ↑i ≠ 1 →
        ∀ (t : Nat),
          have p := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i;
          have k := Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t;
          have C := Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k;
          ∃ q base w,
            base =
                7 ^ k +
                  (p.entrance.outgoing.inner.moves.length + q * p.entrance.outgoing.row.phases.size +
                      ↑p.entrance.outgoing.row.selected +
                    3) ∧
              Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                  Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                    (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q)
                    ((Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i).initial (k - 4)).toState ∧
                p.CycleIndex t q ∧
                  Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w ∧
                    Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + w.length) =
                        Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!p.entrance.outgoing.row.positive) C
                          (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary p.entrance.outgoing q)
                          ((Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i).output (k - 4)) ∧
                      ↑w.length = (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.ladder i).moves (k - 4) ∧
                        ∀ (j : Nat),
                          j ≤ w.length → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + j) D ≤ 55 + 6 * (k - 4)
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.reached_ordinary_ladder' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.scale_parameters
Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.scale_parameters : ∀ (k : Nat),
  6 ≤ k → Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent (Heraclitus.ScaleSchedule.index (k - 6)) ((k - 6) / 36) = k
'Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.scale_parameters' depends on axioms: [propext, Quot.sound]

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.actual_crossing_start
Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.actual_crossing_start : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36) (t q : Nat),
      (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).CycleIndex t q →
        Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.crossingIndex i t q) =
          Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!(Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing.row.positive)
            (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t))
            (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q)
            (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.initial (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing.geometry.other
                (↑(7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t))
                (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.data.anchor).toState
'Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.actual_crossing_start' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.all_phase_crossing_offsets
Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.all_phase_crossing_offsets : ∀ (i : Fin 36),
  have r := (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing;
  have delta :=
    ↑r.inner.moves.length + r.row.winningCycle * r.row.model.period + ↑↑r.row.selected + 1 - 3 * r.row.scale;
  -1 ≤ delta ∧ delta ≤ 12
'Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.all_phase_crossing_offsets' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.original_rule_return_geometry
Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.original_rule_return_geometry : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36) (t q n : Nat),
      (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).CycleIndex t q →
        Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.crossingIndex i t q ≤ n →
          n ≤ 7 ^ (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t + 1) →
            4 * ↑(7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t) - 1 ≤ ↑n ∧
              Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t) - 4096 ≤ ↑(a n).natAbs ∧
                ↑(a n).natAbs ≤ Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t + 1) + 4096

 Quot.sound]

  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (k n : Nat),
      6 ≤ k →
        7 ^ k < n →
          n ≤ 7 ^ (k + 1) →
            Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k - 4096 ≤ ↑(a n).natAbs ∧
              ↑(a n).natAbs ≤ Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (k + 1) + 4096

END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.two_moves_valid
Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.two_moves_valid : ∀ (old other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary) (side : Bool) (k : Nat)
  (C A : Int),
  2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
    100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
      -3 ≤ A ∧ A ≤ 3 →
        -100 ≤ old.front ∧ old.front < 100 →
          (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ old.extra → x < 100) →
            3 * ↑(7 ^ k) - 200 ≤ other.front →
              Heraclitus.SignedValidWord
                (Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                  (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.initial old (↑(7 ^ k)) A).toState)
                (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) [2 * ↑(7 ^ k) + A, ↑(7 ^ k) + A])
'Heraclitus.UpperReturnGeometry.two_moves_valid' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_approach
Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_approach : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.ApproachWord.Checked),
  c ∈ Heraclitus.ApproachWordData.catalogue →
    ∀ (t : Nat) (C : Int) (side : Bool) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
      2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ c.row.exponent t) →
        3 * c.row.scale t - 200 ≤ other.front →
          ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
            Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
              ∀ (base : Nat),
                Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                    Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other (c.row.start (c.row.scale t)) →
                  ∃ w,
                    Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w ∧
                      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + w.length) =
                          Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other (c.row.finish (c.row.scale t)) ∧
                        ↑w.length = c.row.moves (c.row.scale t) ∧
                          ∀ (i : Nat), i ≤ w.length → ∀ (D : Nat), Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 44
'Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_approach' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_post
Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_post : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36),
      ↑i ≠ 1 →
        ∀ (t q base : Nat),
          Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
              Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!(Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing.row.positive)
                (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t))
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q)
                ((Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).endpoint t) →
            3 * ↑(7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t) - 200 ≤
                (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q).front →
              ∃ length,
                Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + length) =
                    Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay
                      (!(Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing.row.positive)
                      (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t))
                      (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q)
                      ((Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.approach i).row.start
                        ((Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.approach i).row.scale t)) ∧
                  Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.TailInterval a base length 56
'Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_post' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

    ∀ (t q base : Nat),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
          Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!(Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase ⟨1, ⋯⟩).entrance.outgoing.row.positive)

            (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase ⟨1, ⋯⟩).entrance.outgoing q)
            ((Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase ⟨1, ⋯⟩).endpoint t) →

            (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase ⟨1, ⋯⟩).entrance.outgoing q).front →
          ∃ length,
            Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + length) =
                Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay
                  (!(Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase ⟨1, ⋯⟩).entrance.outgoing.row.positive)
                  (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent ⟨1, ⋯⟩ t))
                  (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase ⟨1, ⋯⟩).entrance.outgoing q)
                  ((Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.approach ⟨1, ⋯⟩).row.start
                    ((Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.approach ⟨1, ⋯⟩).row.scale t)) ∧
              Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.TailInterval a base length 56

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_transfer
Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_transfer : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.SignedTransfer.Checked),
  c.original ∈ Heraclitus.TransferCatalogue.catalogue →
    ∀ (k : Nat) (C d h : Int) (later : List Int) (side : Bool) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary),
      2 * C + 1 = ↑(7 ^ k) →
        100000 ≤ 7 ^ k →
          2401 ≤ h →
            h % ↑c.original.transfer.modulus = c.original.transfer.residue →
              (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ later → 2 * h ≤ x) →
                0 ≤ d →
                  d + h ≤ 2 * ↑(7 ^ k) + 3 →
                    3 * ↑(7 ^ k) - 200 ≤ other.front →
                      ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
                        Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
                          ∀ (base : Nat),
                            Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                                Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                                  ((Heraclitus.SignedTransfer.start c.original.transfer h later).shift d).toState →
                              ∃ q w,
                                Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base) w ∧
                                  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (base + w.length) =
                                      Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                                        (Heraclitus.translate d
                                          (Heraclitus.SignedTransfer.endpoint c.original.transfer h later)) ∧
                                    h = c.original.transfer.target + ↑q * ↑c.original.transfer.modulus ∧
                                      w.length =
                                          c.original.transfer.transient.edges.length + c.original.transfer.modulus * q +
                                              c.original.transfer.phase +
                                            c.original.transfer.repair.edges.length ∧
                                        ∀ (i : Nat),
                                          i ≤ w.length →
                                            ∀ (D : Nat),
                                              Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 54 + later.length
'Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.actual_transfer' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

              (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q)
              ((Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).endpoint t) →
          3 * ↑(7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t) - 200 ≤
              (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q).front →
            ∃ length,
              base + length = 7 ^ (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t + 1) ∧
                Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.TailInterval a base length 56

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.post_approach
Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.post_approach : ∀ (i : Fin 36),
  ↑i ≠ 1 →
    ∀ (t : Nat),
      (Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.approach i).row.start ((Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.approach i).row.scale t) =
        (Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.post i).input.endpoint t
'Heraclitus.UpperReturnTail.post_approach' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.actual_core_start
Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.actual_core_start : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (i : Fin 36) (t q : Nat),
      (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).CycleIndex t q →
        Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a (Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.crossingIndex i t q + 2) =
            Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay (!(Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing.row.positive)
              (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t))
              (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q)
              (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.checkpoint (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing.geometry.other
                  (↑(7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t))
                  (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.data.anchor).toState ∧
          3 * ↑(7 ^ Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.exponent i t) - 200 ≤
            (Heraclitus.ReturnBridge.farBoundary (Heraclitus.UpperPhaseBridge.phase i).entrance.outgoing q).front
'Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.actual_core_start' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
