BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.return_defect
Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.return_defect : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    ∀ (k n : Nat),
      6 ≤ k →
        n ≤ 7 ^ (k + 1) →
          4 * ↑(7 ^ k) - 1 ≤ ↑n →
            Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius k - 4096 ≤ ↑(a n).natAbs →
              ↑(a n).natAbs ≤ Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (k + 1) + 4096 →
                Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a n (Heraclitus.ScaleInduction.radius (k + 1)).toNat ≤ 55 + 6 * (k - 4) →
                  Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a n ≤ 32771 + 6 * (↑k - 4)
'Heraclitus.UpperScaleAssembly.return_defect' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_catalogue_tail
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_catalogue_tail : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (base D : Nat) (side : Bool) (C : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary)
      (c : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.CheckedTransfer),
      c ∈ Heraclitus.TransferCatalogue.catalogue →
        ∀ (q : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
          h = c.transfer.target + ↑q * ↑c.transfer.modulus →
            Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                  (Heraclitus.mark c.transfer.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x) →
              Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base)
                  (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) (c.transfer.word q h)) →
                0 < C + c.transfer.initial.toState.current →
                  (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ c.transfer.word q h → 0 < C + x) →
                    ∀ (markers : List Int),
                      (∀ (x : Int), x = h ∨ later x → x ∈ markers) →
                        ∀ (i : Nat),
                          i ≤ (c.transfer.word q h).length →
                            Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ 53 + markers.length
'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_catalogue_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_tail
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_tail : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (n D : Nat) (side : Bool) (C : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary) (s : Heraclitus.State),
      Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a n = Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other s →
        0 < C + s.current →
          ∀ (K : Nat) (M : Int → Prop) (markers : List Int),
            Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.Covered K M s →
              (∀ (x : Int), M x → x ∈ markers) → Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a n D ≤ K + markers.length
'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_transfer_tail
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_transfer_tail : ∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.IsWalk a →
    ∀ (base D : Nat) (side : Bool) (C : Int) (other : Heraclitus.RayBoundary)
      (t : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.Transfer),
      t.Claim →
        ∀ (K q : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
          h = t.target + ↑q * ↑t.modulus →
            Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.transient K →
              Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.cycle K →
                Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.before K →
                  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.repair K →
                    Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base =
                        Heraclitus.OutgoingWord.liftRay side C other
                          (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x) →
                      Heraclitus.SignedValidWord (Heraclitus.GlobalWalk.stateAt a base)
                          (List.map (Heraclitus.embedRay side C) (t.word q h)) →
                        0 < C + t.initial.toState.current →
                          (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ t.word q h → 0 < C + x) →
                            ∀ (markers : List Int),
                              (∀ (x : Int), x = h ∨ later x → x ∈ markers) →
                                ∀ (i : Nat),
                                  i ≤ (t.word q h).length →
                                    Heraclitus.PhysicalTail.count a (base + i) D ≤ K + markers.length
'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.actual_transfer_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.append
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.append : ∀ (K : Nat) (M : Int → Prop) (s : Heraclitus.State) (u v : List Int),

    Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K M (Heraclitus.execute s u) v →

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.catalogue_prefixes

  c ∈ Heraclitus.TransferCatalogue.catalogue →
    ∀ (q : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
      h = c.transfer.target + ↑q * ↑c.transfer.modulus →
        Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered 53 (fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
          (Heraclitus.mark c.transfer.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x) (c.transfer.word q h)
'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.catalogue_prefixes' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.catalogue_uniform
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.catalogue_uniform : ∀ (c : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.CheckedTransfer),
  c ∈ Heraclitus.TransferCatalogue.catalogue →
    ∀ (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
      2401 ≤ h →
        h % ↑c.transfer.modulus = c.transfer.residue →
          (∀ (x : Int), later x → 2 * h ≤ x) →
            ∃ q,
              h = c.transfer.target + ↑q * ↑c.transfer.modulus ∧
                Heraclitus.ValidWord (Heraclitus.mark c.transfer.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                    (c.transfer.word q h) ∧
                  Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark c.transfer.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                        (c.transfer.word q h) =
                      Heraclitus.mark (Heraclitus.translate h c.transfer.output.toState) later ∧
                    Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered 53 (fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                      (Heraclitus.mark c.transfer.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x) (c.transfer.word q h)
'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.catalogue_uniform' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END

    (∀ (x : Int), M x → x ∈ markers) →
      xs.Nodup → (∀ (x : Int), x ∈ xs → s.current < x ∧ s.used x) → xs.length ≤ K + markers.length

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.cycles

  Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound r K →
    Heraclitus.execute (r.initial.at f).toState (r.word f) = Heraclitus.translate d (r.initial.at f).toState →
      Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K M (Heraclitus.mark (r.initial.at f).toState M)
        (Heraclitus.repeatedWord (r.word f) d q)

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.transfer

  t.Claim →
    ∀ (K q : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
      h = t.target + ↑q * ↑t.modulus →
        Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.transient K →
          Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.cycle K →
            Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.before K →
              Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.repair K →
                Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K (fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                  (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x) (t.word q h)

BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.transfer_tail
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.transfer_tail : ∀ (t : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.Transfer),
  t.Claim →
    ∀ (K q : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
      h = t.target + ↑q * ↑t.modulus →
        Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.transient K →
          Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.cycle K →
            Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.before K →
              Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.repair K →
                ∀ (markers : List Int),
                  (∀ (x : Int), x = h ∨ later x → x ∈ markers) →
                    ∀ (i : Nat),
                      i ≤ (t.word q h).length →
                        ∀ (xs : List Int),
                          xs.Nodup →
                            (∀ (x : Int),
                                x ∈ xs →
                                  (Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                                          (List.take i (t.word q h))).current <
                                      x ∧
                                    (Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                                          (List.take i (t.word q h))).used
                                      x) →
                              xs.length ≤ K + markers.length
'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.transfer_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.transfer_tower_tail
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.transfer_tower_tail : ∀ (t : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.Transfer),
  t.Claim →
    ∀ (q : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
      h = t.target + ↑q * ↑t.modulus →
        Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.transient 53 →
          Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.cycle 53 →
            Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.before 53 →
              Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.RouteBound t.repair 53 →
                ∀ (H shift outer₁ outer₂ : Int) (levels : Nat),
                  (∀ (x : Int),
                      x = h ∨ later x → Heraclitus.LadderLayers.tower H levels (x - shift) ∨ x = outer₁ ∨ x = outer₂) →
                    ∀ (i : Nat),
                      i ≤ (t.word q h).length →
                        ∀ (xs : List Int),
                          xs.Nodup →
                            (∀ (x : Int),
                                x ∈ xs →
                                  (Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                                          (List.take i (t.word q h))).current <
                                      x ∧
                                    (Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                                          (List.take i (t.word q h))).used
                                      x) →
                              xs.length ≤ 55 + 6 * levels
'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.transfer_tower_tail' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.uniform
Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.uniform : ∀ (t : Heraclitus.TransferCertificate.Transfer),
  t.Claim →
    ∀ (K : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
      2401 ≤ h →
        h % ↑t.modulus = t.residue →
          (∀ (x : Int), later x → 2 * h ≤ x) →

                          Heraclitus.execute (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x) (t.word q h) =
                              Heraclitus.mark (Heraclitus.translate h t.output.toState) later ∧
                            Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.AllCovered K (fun x => x = h ∨ later x)
                              (Heraclitus.mark t.initial.toState fun x => x = h ∨ later x) (t.word q h)

END
BEGIN Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.uniform_from_nodes

    ∀ (K : Nat) (h : Int) (later : Int → Prop),
      2401 ≤ h →
        h % ↑t.modulus = t.residue →

'Heraclitus.UpperTransferPrefix.uniform_from_nodes' depends on axioms: [propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]
END
def Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect : (Nat → Int) → Nat → Int :=
fun a n => 4 * Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.twiceDeviation a n - 3 * ↑n
def Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.EventualUpperBound : (Nat → Int) → Prop :=
fun a =>
  ∀ (p q : Nat),
    0 < p → 0 < q → ∃ N, ∀ (n : Nat), N ≤ n → 0 < n → ↑q * Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a n ≤ 8 * ↑p * ↑n
def Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.FrequentLowerBound : (Nat → Int) → Prop :=
fun a =>
  ∀ (p q : Nat),
    0 < p → 0 < q → ∀ (N : Nat), ∃ n, N ≤ n ∧ 0 < n ∧ -(8 * ↑p * ↑n) ≤ ↑q * Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.defect a n
def Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.OriginalRuleThreeEighths : Prop :=
∀ (a : Nat → Int),
  Heraclitus.OriginalProblem.FollowsRule a →
    Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.EventualUpperBound a ∧ Heraclitus.UpperMachineAudit.FrequentLowerBound a
